# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - FALLOUT WORLD END EVENTS
# ============================================================================
# Owns Fallout presentation, Air Winter event chains, and later Fallout world
# content. Fallout request and rewrite control must remain outside zombie files.
# ============================================================================

add_namespace = chaosx.fallout

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Air Winter phase 1 regional entries
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.1
	title = chaosx.fallout.1.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.1.a
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_thermometer_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_thermometer_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_boreal_shifts
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_boreal_shift_network
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_boreal_shift_network
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.1.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.1.b
		trigger = {
			air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_has_operational_factory = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_full_shift_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_full_shift_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_war = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_government = fascism
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_has_operational_factory = yes }
				}
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_war_support = constant:air_winter_event_country.war_support_moderate
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_minor }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_boreal_full
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_boreal_full_shifts
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_boreal_full_shifts
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.1.b.tt
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.2
	title = chaosx.fallout.2.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.2.a
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_warehouse_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_warehouse_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_minor }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_maritime_warehouses
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_dry_warehouse_rooms
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_dry_warehouse_rooms
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.2.a.tt
	}

	option = {

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_shipyard_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_shipyard_ai_is_plausible = yes } }

					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes

					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_maritime_shipyards
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_shipyard_coal_priority
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_shipyard_coal_priority
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.3
	title = chaosx.fallout.3.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.3.a
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_cistern_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_cistern_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_water_security = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.water_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_minor }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_arid_cisterns
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_cistern_rationing
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_cistern_rationing
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.3.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.3.b
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_fountain_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_fountain_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = neutrality
					has_stability < constant:air_winter_event_ai.low_stability
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_moderate
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_water_security = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.water_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_arid_fountains
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_open_fountains
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_open_fountains
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.3.b.tt
	}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.4
	title = chaosx.fallout.4.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.4.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.4.a
		trigger = {
			air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes
			air_winter_event_phase_1_can_pay_drainage = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_drainage_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_drainage_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = communism
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_has_low_food = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes
					air_winter_event_phase_1_can_pay_drainage = yes
				}
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate_cost
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_minor }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_tropical_drainage
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_cold_rain_drainage
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_cold_rain_drainage
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.4.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.4.b
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_school_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_school_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_stability < constant:air_winter_event_ai.low_stability
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_minor }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_tropical_schools
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_school_shelters
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_school_shelters
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.4.b.tt
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.5
	title = chaosx.fallout.5.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.5.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_1
	is_triggered_only = yes

				}
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_command_power = constant:air_winter_event_country.command_power_minor_cost
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_moderate }

					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_highland_markers
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_route_markers
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_route_markers
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.5.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.5.b
		trigger = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_phase_1_recall_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_phase_1_recall_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = neutrality
					has_government = democratic
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_phase_1_opening_targets_are_valid = yes }
				air_winter_phase_1_clear_country_memory = yes
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_phase_1_clear_opening_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_minor }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_phase_1_chain_highland_recall
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_recalled_road_crews
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_recalled_road_crews
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.6 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_opening_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.5.b.tt
	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Air Winter phase 2 regional entries

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.10
	title = chaosx.fallout.10.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.10.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_2
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.10.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_major } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_has_low_food = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_seed_vault_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_seed_vault_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_major }
				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_event_clear_seed_ledger_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_severe }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_seed_chain_vaults
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_seed_vaults
					air_winter_event_apply_seed_vault_industry = yes
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_major_cost
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_long_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.18 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.10.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.10.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_event_clear_seed_ledger_memory = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_emergency }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_seed_chain_herds

				add_war_support = constant:air_winter_event_country.war_support_minor

	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.10.c

			}
			modifier = {

				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes

				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.18 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.10.c.tt
	}

				has_state_flag = air_winter_seed_chain_vaults

			}

		name = chaosx.fallout.18.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {

				air_winter_event_seed_vault_succeeds = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
						has_state_flag = air_winter_pending_delayed_result
						has_state_flag = air_winter_seed_chain_vaults
						air_winter_event_seed_vault_succeeds = yes
					}
				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_event_clear_seed_vault_industry = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_minor }

					clr_state_flag = air_winter_memory_tunnel_school_protection
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_tunnel_civic_failure
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.17.b.tt
	}

	option = {

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.38
	title = chaosx.fallout.38.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				naval_base > constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero
			}
		}
		text = chaosx.fallout.38.harbor.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				NOT = { naval_base > constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero }
			}
		}
		text = chaosx.fallout.38.anchorage.d
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_island_refugee_harbor
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		air_winter_island_refugee_offer_is_valid = yes
		event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
			air_winter_event_is_engine_island_state = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.38.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { convoy_1 < constant:air_winter_event_country.island_refugee_convoy_cost } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = neutrality
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_event_island_refugee_rescue_ai_is_plausible = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_has_low_food = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_has_low_shelter = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					NOT = { air_winter_event_island_refugee_rescue_ai_is_plausible = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_island_refugee_offer_is_valid = yes
					NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { convoy_1 < constant:air_winter_event_country.island_refugee_convoy_cost } }
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
						air_winter_event_is_engine_island_state = yes
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_island_refugee_applied = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_island_refugee_share = constant:air_winter_event_island_refugee.rescue_population_share }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_island_refugee_cap = constant:air_winter_event_island_refugee.rescue_population_ceiling }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_island_refugee_source_pressure_relief = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.refugees_major }
				air_winter_island_refugee_transfer_population = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = air_winter_island_refugee_applied
							value = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero
							compare = greater_than
						}
					}
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
						air_winter_island_refugee_clear_pending_branch = yes
						set_state_flag = air_winter_island_refugee_chain_rescue
						subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_major }
						subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
						add_to_variable = { air_winter_refugee_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.refugees_major }
						add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_moderate }
						add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_minor }
						set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_island_refugee_rescue
						set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_island_refugee_voluntary_entry
						air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
					}
					event_target:air_winter_refugee_source_state = {
						set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_island_refugee_departure_rescue
					}
					event_target:air_winter_refugee_source_country = {
						set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_island_refugee_departure_rescue
					}
					set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_island_refugee_open_shore
					add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate_cost
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_response_payment = constant:air_winter_event_country.island_refugee_convoy_cost }
					air_winter_response_pay_convoys = yes
					add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_major_loss
					air_winter_island_refugee_commit_offer = yes
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_standard_days }
					country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.39 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
				}
				else = { air_winter_island_refugee_rollback_offer = yes }
			}
			else = { air_winter_island_refugee_rollback_offer = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.38.a.tt
	}

		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.39.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				air_winter_event_has_island_refugee_rescue_branch = yes

					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_moderate }

					air_winter_island_refugee_clear_pending_branch = yes
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_moderate_loss

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.20
	title = chaosx.fallout.20.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.20.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_3
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.20.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_rail_clearance
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate_cost
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_standard_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.21 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.20.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.20.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_war = no
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_minor }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_roads
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_road_priority
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_command_power = constant:air_winter_event_country.command_power_minor_cost
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_standard_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.21 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.20.b.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.20.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.risky
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_stability < constant:air_winter_event_ai.low_stability
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.21
	title = chaosx.fallout.21.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
				check_variable = { air_winter_reclamation > constant:air_winter_event_outcome.rail_reclamation_threshold }
				check_variable = { air_winter_phase < constant:air_winter_event_outcome.rail_phase_ceiling }
			}
		}
		text = chaosx.fallout.21.rail_success.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { air_winter_reclamation > constant:air_winter_event_outcome.rail_reclamation_threshold } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { air_winter_phase < constant:air_winter_event_outcome.rail_phase_ceiling } }
				}
			}
		}
		text = chaosx.fallout.21.rail_failure.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:air_winter_event_state = { has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_roads } }
		text = chaosx.fallout.21.roads.d
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:air_winter_event_state = { has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_abandoned } }
		text = chaosx.fallout.21.abandoned.d
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_3
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
		event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
				has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_roads
				has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_abandoned
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.21.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {

			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
						has_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
					}
				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					set_variable = { air_winter_rail_protection_months = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.rail_protection_months }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_moderate }
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_route_chain_rail
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_rail_clearance_succeeded
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}

	}

				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_heat_chain_barracks
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_barracks_heat
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_war_support = constant:air_winter_event_country.war_support_moderate
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.24 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.23.b.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.23.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.risky
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_stability > constant:air_winter_event_ai.high_stability
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.22
	title = chaosx.fallout.22.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.22.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_3
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
		event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_is_hydroelectric_state = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.22.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate }
			NOT = { command_power < constant:air_winter_event_country.command_power_minor }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < constant:air_winter_event_country.support_equipment_crew_cost } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_dam_ice_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_dam_ice_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					NOT = { has_manpower < constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate }
					NOT = { command_power < constant:air_winter_event_country.command_power_minor }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < constant:air_winter_event_country.support_equipment_crew_cost } }
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_is_hydroelectric_state = yes }
				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_minor }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_water_security = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.water_minor }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_dam_chain_ice_crews
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_dam_ice_crews
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_moderate_cost
				add_command_power = constant:air_winter_event_country.command_power_minor_cost
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_response_payment = constant:air_winter_event_country.support_equipment_crew_cost }
				air_winter_response_pay_support_equipment = yes
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_standard_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.25 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.22.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.22.b
		ai_chance = {

				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_minor }
					air_winter_event_apply_refinery_output_disruption = yes
					set_state_flag = air_winter_refinery_chain_shutdown
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_refinery_shutdown
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_short_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.27 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.26.a.tt
	}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.33
	title = chaosx.fallout.33.t

	trigger = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.33.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_has_low_shelter = yes }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_severe }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_harvest_chain_burned
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_burned_stalks
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_standard_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.32 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }

					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_ash_city_chain_crews
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_protected_work_crews
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_manpower = constant:air_winter_event_country.manpower_major_cost
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_long_days }

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.47
	title = chaosx.fallout.47.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.47.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_dead_city_salvage
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
		event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
			air_winter_event_is_dead_city_salvage_candidate = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.47.a
		trigger = { air_winter_event_dead_city_can_pay_survey = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_dead_city_survey_ai_is_plausible = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.weak_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = { NOT = { air_winter_event_dead_city_survey_ai_is_plausible = yes } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_war = no
				OR = {
					has_government = democratic
					has_government = neutrality
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					air_winter_event_dead_city_can_pay_survey = yes
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_is_dead_city_salvage_candidate = yes }
				}
				air_winter_dead_city_clear_country_memory = yes
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					air_winter_dead_city_clear_state_policy_and_outcome_memory = yes

		trigger = { event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_has_dead_city_licensed_branch = yes air_winter_event_dead_city_licensed_is_partial = yes } }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_dead_city_result_targets_are_valid = yes event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_has_dead_city_licensed_branch = yes air_winter_event_dead_city_licensed_is_partial = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_dead_city_infantry_grant = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_dead_city_support_grant = constant:air_winter_event_dead_city.licensed_partial_support }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_dead_city_motorized_grant = constant:air_winter_event_dead_city.licensed_partial_motorized }
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_dead_city_train_grant = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.zero }
				air_winter_dead_city_grant_salvage_equipment = yes
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_death_percent = constant:air_winter_event_deaths.dead_city_licensed_partial_percent }
					air_winter_event_apply_deaths = yes
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_moderate }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_dead_city_salvage_partial
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_dead_city_salvage_casualties
					air_winter_dead_city_finalize_result = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_dead_city_salvage_partial
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_dead_city_salvage_casualties
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}

	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.48.i
		trigger = { event_target:air_winter_event_state = { air_winter_event_has_dead_city_licensed_branch = yes air_winter_event_dead_city_licensed_is_disaster = yes NOT = { air_winter_event_dead_city_altered_return_is_ready = yes } } }

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.43
	title = chaosx.fallout.43.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.43.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_6
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.43.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_stability > constant:air_winter_event_ai.high_stability
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_moderate }
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_death_percent = constant:air_winter_event_deaths.terminal_order_percent }
					air_winter_event_apply_deaths = yes
					set_state_flag = air_winter_terminal_chain_public
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_public_continuity
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_major
				air_winter_event_refresh_country_cooldown = yes
				set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_delay_days = constant:air_winter_event_runtime.delayed_long_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.46 days = air_winter_event_delay_days }
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.43.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.43.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				has_stability < constant:air_winter_event_ai.low_stability
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_extreme }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_refugee_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.refugees_extreme }
					set_state_flag = air_winter_terminal_chain_decentralized
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_decentralized_continuity
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}

			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.43.b.tt
	}

				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:air_winter_event_outcome.infrastructure_damage }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.disease_moderate }
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_thaw_chain_fields
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_thaw_chain_roads
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_thaw_chain_water
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_false_thaw
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.51.b.tt
	}
}

				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_minor }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_minor }
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_second_chain_military
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_second_military_held
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_war_support = constant:air_winter_event_country.war_support_minor
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.61.e.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.61.f
		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
				has_state_flag = air_winter_second_chain_military
				NOT = { AND = { check_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure < constant:air_winter_event_outcome.second_winter_building_pressure_ceiling } check_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity > constant:air_winter_event_outcome.second_winter_ledger_threshold } } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					air_winter_event_targets_are_valid = yes
					event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
						has_state_flag = air_winter_second_chain_military
						NOT = { AND = { check_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure < constant:air_winter_event_outcome.second_winter_building_pressure_ceiling } check_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity > constant:air_winter_event_outcome.second_winter_ledger_threshold } } }
					}
				}
				event_target:air_winter_event_state = {
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.building_pressure_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_category_damage = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.category_damage_major }
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_death_percent = constant:air_winter_event_deaths.second_winter_failure_percent }
					air_winter_event_apply_deaths = yes
					air_winter_update_building_damage = yes
					if = { limit = { air_winter_state_category_can_degrade = yes } air_winter_degrade_state_category = yes }
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_second_chain_military
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_second_military_fractured
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
				}
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
			}
			else = { air_winter_event_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.61.f.tt
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.201
	title = chaosx.fallout.201.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.201.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_5
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { air_winter_response_abandonment_target_is_valid = yes }

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.201.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					check_variable = {
						var = air_winter_survival_value
						value = constant:air_winter_response_result.abandonment_hold_minimum_survival
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_response_abandonment_target_is_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_memory_withdrawal_council
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_outskirts_held
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_extreme }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_major }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_food_reserve = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.food_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.exposure_major }
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
					air_winter_response_clear_pending_result = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = air_winter_memory_abandonment_refused
				add_war_support = constant:air_winter_event_country.war_support_major
			}
			else = { air_winter_response_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.201.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.201.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.secondary
			modifier = {
				factor = constant:air_winter_event_ai.strong_modifier
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = { air_winter_has_low_shelter = yes }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_response_abandonment_target_is_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_memory_outskirts_held
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_withdrawal_council
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_adaptation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.adaptation_major }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.reclamation_moderate }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_shelter_capacity = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.shelter_moderate }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_refugee_pressure = constant:air_winter_event_ledger.refugees_major }
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
					air_winter_response_clear_pending_result = yes
				}
				clr_country_flag = air_winter_memory_abandonment_refused
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor
			}
			else = { air_winter_response_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}

	}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.202
	title = chaosx.fallout.202.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.202.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_air_winter_phase_5
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {

		trigger = {
			event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
				check_variable = {
					var = air_winter_survival_value
					value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_survival
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = air_winter_adaptation
					value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_adaptation
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = air_winter_water_security
					value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_water
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_response_decontamination_target_is_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_memory_decontamination_failed
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_decontamination_succeeded
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_exposure_reduction }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_disease_reduction }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_water_security = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_water_cost }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_reclamation = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_reclamation_gain }
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
					air_winter_response_clear_pending_result = yes
				}
			}
			else = { air_winter_response_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.202.a.tt
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.202.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					check_variable = {
						var = air_winter_survival_value
						value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_survival
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = air_winter_adaptation
						value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_adaptation
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = air_winter_water_security
						value = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_minimum_water
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = constant:air_winter_event_ai.primary }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { air_winter_response_decontamination_target_is_valid = yes }
				event_target:air_winter_response_state = {
					clr_state_flag = air_winter_memory_decontamination_succeeded
					set_state_flag = air_winter_memory_decontamination_failed
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_exposure = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_failure_exposure }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_disease_pressure = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_failure_disease }
					add_to_variable = { air_winter_building_damage_pressure = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_failure_building_pressure }
					subtract_from_variable = { air_winter_water_security = constant:air_winter_response_result.decontamination_water_cost }
					set_temp_variable = { air_winter_event_death_percent = constant:air_winter_event_deaths.decontamination_failure_percent }
					air_winter_event_apply_deaths = yes
					air_winter_event_refresh_state = yes
					air_winter_response_clear_pending_result = yes
				}
				add_stability = constant:air_winter_event_country.stability_minor_loss
			}
			else = { air_winter_response_reject_stale_choice = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = chaosx.fallout.202.b.tt
	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# New Zealand Lifeboat State country-memory pilot

# Suffixes 127 through 152 are manually written dormant content. Focuses in the
# generation-bound NZL package are their only callers. Suffixes 100 through 126
# remain reserved for the Fallout living-world scheduler.

# Harbor Law opening chain
country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.127
	title = chaosx.fallout.127.t
	desc = chaosx.fallout.127.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_fallout_nzl_opening
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_ai = no
		fallout_nzl_lifeboat_package_is_current = yes
		NOT = { fallout_nzl_opening_chain_is_current = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = fallout_nzl_opening_chain_active
		set_variable = { fallout_nzl_opening_chain_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_choice
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_result
	}

	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.127.a
		trigger = { NOT = { has_political_power < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.political_power_low } }
		set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.open_ledger }
		fallout_nzl_pay_opening_choice_cost = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.short_delay_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.130 days = fallout_nzl_event_delay_days }
		ai_chance = { base = constant:fallout_nzl_ai.normal }
	}
	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.127.b
		trigger = { NOT = { command_power < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.command_power_low } }
		set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.guarded_berths }
		fallout_nzl_pay_opening_choice_cost = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.short_delay_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.130 days = fallout_nzl_event_delay_days }
		ai_chance = { base = constant:fallout_nzl_ai.normal }
	}
	option = {
		name = chaosx.fallout.127.c
		set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.local_compacts }
		fallout_nzl_pay_opening_choice_cost = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.short_delay_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.130 days = fallout_nzl_event_delay_days }
		ai_chance = { base = constant:fallout_nzl_ai.normal }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.128
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_ai = yes
		fallout_nzl_lifeboat_package_is_current = yes
		NOT = { fallout_nzl_opening_chain_is_current = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = fallout_nzl_opening_chain_active
		set_variable = { fallout_nzl_opening_chain_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_choice
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_result
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = yes
				NOT = { command_power < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.command_power_low }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.guarded_berths }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { fallout_nzl_parliament_trust < constant:fallout_nzl_value.strained }
				NOT = { has_political_power < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.political_power_low }
			}
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.open_ledger }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.local_compacts } }
		fallout_nzl_pay_opening_choice_cost = yes
		set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.short_delay_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.130 days = fallout_nzl_event_delay_days }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.130
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { always = yes }
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_nzl_lifeboat_package_is_current = yes
				fallout_nzl_opening_chain_is_current = yes
			}
			fallout_nzl_calculate_opening_result = yes
			fallout_nzl_apply_opening_result = yes
			fallout_nzl_record_opening_event_log = yes
			if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.129 } }
			else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.132 } }
		}
		else = { fallout_nzl_close_opening_chain = yes }
	}
}

		event_target:fallout_nzl_external_partner_target = {

		}

		fallout_nzl_pay_external_choice_cost = yes

	id = chaosx.fallout.140
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { is_ai = yes fallout_nzl_lifeboat_package_is_current = yes fallout_nzl_external_chain_is_current = yes }
	immediate = {
		save_global_event_target_as = fallout_nzl_external_owner_target
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_choice
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_chain_result
		clear_variable = fallout_nzl_partner_response

			limit = { fallout_nzl_external_no_partner_is_current = yes }
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.local_compacts }
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.failure }

			country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.144 days = fallout_nzl_event_delay_days }
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = fallout_nzl_humanitarian_route
					NOT = { has_equipment = { convoy_1 < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.convoy_low } }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.open_ledger }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = fallout_nzl_isolation_route
					NOT = { has_political_power < constant:fallout_nzl_cost.political_power_medium }
				}
				set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.guarded_berths }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.local_compacts } }
			fallout_nzl_pay_external_choice_cost = yes
			event_target:fallout_nzl_external_partner_target = {
				if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.141 } }
				else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.142 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

		event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = {
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.partial }

			set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.standard_delay_days }

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.142
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_ai = yes
		has_event_target = fallout_nzl_external_owner_target
		event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = { fallout_nzl_external_transaction_is_current = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target }
			set_country_flag = fallout_nzl_partner_rejected_passage
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = {
				set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.failure }
				fallout_nzl_record_hostile_external_rejection = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic }
			set_country_flag = fallout_nzl_partner_accepted_passage
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = { set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.success } }
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = fallout_nzl_partner_restricted_passage
			set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response_generation = global.fallout_transition_generation }
			event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = { set_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.partial } }
		}
		event_target:fallout_nzl_external_owner_target = {
			set_temp_variable = { fallout_nzl_event_delay_days = constant:fallout_nzl_event.standard_delay_days }

country_event = {
	id = chaosx.fallout.144
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { always = yes }
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				fallout_nzl_lifeboat_package_is_current = yes
				fallout_nzl_external_chain_is_current = yes
			}
			fallout_nzl_calculate_external_result = yes
			fallout_nzl_apply_external_result = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.143 }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = fallout_nzl_isolation_route
						fallout_nzl_external_transaction_is_current = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = fallout_nzl_foreign_basing_ever_granted }
						check_variable = { fallout_nzl_sea_lane_security < constant:fallout_nzl_value.stable }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.partial }
							check_variable = { fallout_nzl_partner_response = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_result.success }
						}
					}
					fallout_nzl_grant_foreign_basing_concession = yes
				}
				country_event = { id = chaosx.fallout.146 }
			}
		}
		else = { fallout_nzl_close_external_chain = yes }
	}
}

		else = { set_variable = { fallout_nzl_chain_choice = constant:fallout_nzl_chain_choice.local_compacts } }
